Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on mol_bio structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 889. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 131 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 889 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mol_bio structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 131. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
